<a id="pm-s4"></a>
## 4. Engineering Triptych

### Panel 1 - epistemic contract

- **invariant-completion-halving**: Real rank zero of the uniform tracial completion ultrapower supplies a central projection that halves every designated coefficient trace under the stated algebra hypotheses.
- **invariant-finite-set-splitting**: Finite unitary tests admit a projection with uniformly small tracial commutators and coefficient halving errors.
- **invariant-unital-absorption**: Strict comparison implies Jiang–Su absorption in the stated simple unital nuclear regime. The tracial case also supplies uniform property Gamma.
- **invariant-coefficient-partitions**: Uniform property Gamma supplies central orthogonal projections summing to the unit and dividing every coefficient trace equally.
- **invariant-projectionless-route**: The stably projectionless comparison route yields uniform property Gamma and Jiang–Su absorption when boundedness of the densely finite trace cone and compactness of its normalized base both hold.
- **invariant-bounded-diagonal**: Approximate satisfaction of every finite initial collection of the prescribed tracial polynomial tests yields simultaneous satisfaction in the ultrapower while retaining the fixed variable domains.

### Panel 2 - negative knowledge

- **anti-scalar-traces-only**: Infer uniform property Gamma from equal scalar traces of a projection partition. -> Scalar trace equality omits the coefficient identities required for uniform property Gamma.
- **anti-finite-tests-as-global-splitting**: Treat a projection satisfying the finite unitary tests as an exact central halving projection for the entire completion. -> The finite-set theorem provides approximate estimates for the prescribed tests. The global conclusion requires the additional bounded diagonal and density arguments.
- **anti-empty-traces-as-comparison**: Apply the unital absorption theorem merely because the algebra has no tracial states. -> The stipulated empty trace convention still requires Cuntz comparison with every nonzero positive target.
- **anti-unbounded-nonunital-traces**: Extend the stably projectionless theorem to algebras with unbounded densely finite traces or a noncompact normalized trace base. -> The theorem requires boundedness of the entire densely finite trace cone and compactness of its normalized base; the proof uses these requirements to obtain the quotient unit and normalize generalized limit traces.
- **anti-centering-as-commutation**: Treat a symmetry centered against the separable coefficient algebra as commuting with that coefficient algebra. -> The proposition supplies commutation with the finite-dimensional algebra and coefficient centering against the separable algebra. These are distinct conclusions.
- **anti-growing-observables-in-diagonal**: Use the Gaussian normalized sums directly as bounded diagonal variables. -> The normalized sums have no common norm bound in the construction. The final diagonal argument instead uses positive contractions obtained from continuous partition functions.
- **anti-uniform-dimension-budget**: Assign a uniform finite matrix size or approximation budget to the rank and nuclear approximation stages. -> The source supplies finite approximants for the chosen inputs but explicitly withholds bounds independent of the element, test set, and tolerance.

### Panel 3 - reference implementation and oracle

Applicability: `not_applicable`. The main conclusions concern projections and absorption in infinite-dimensional algebras, obtained through tracial ultrapowers and bounded diagonal existence arguments. The ultrapower construction states: "Fix a free ultrafilter $\omega$ on $\mathbb N$." The diagonal principle concludes: "Then there is an admissible tuple with $t_k(x)\le c_k$ for every $k$." These steps do not specify a finite executable construction of the main outputs. Auxiliary finite matrix calculations would provide only restricted checks of individual estimates, without deciding the ultrapower existence or absorption conclusions.

<a id="pm-s5"></a>
## 5. Domain engineering contracts

| domain | applies | strength | maturity | basis | why |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| software | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The ultrapower projection construction supplies an abstract existence result without a finite representation or executable procedure for general input algebras. |
| games_simulation | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The Gaussian limit concerns coefficient traces of abstract commuting observables; it supplies no simulation dynamics or finite sampling procedure. |
| tools | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The finite-set splitting theorem provides mathematical conditions for approximate commutation and trace halving, but no procedure for presenting the ambient algebra or evaluating the supremum over its traces. |
| algorithms | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The rank approximation constructs finite matrix elements through abstract fullness witnesses, while explicitly leaving their matrix size uncontrolled independently of the input and tolerance. |
| data_structures | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The central sequence quotient and its surjective lifting map describe algebraic objects, without a finite encoding, storage representation, or operations contract. |
| hardware_accelerators | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | Finite-dimensional approximation factors appear as mathematical witnesses, with no bound on the number of outgoing summands independent of the approximation request and no hardware execution construction. |
| crypto_security | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | Strict comparison yields tensorial absorption under structural algebra hypotheses; it provides no cryptographic primitive, adversary model, or security reduction. |
| machine_learning | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The finite-dimensional models approximate operator tuples through weighted tracial errors. Their existence does not specify training data, a learning rule, or statistical generalisation. |
| optimisation_operations_research | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The cost-selection lemma bounds a positive observable while prescribing projection traces, but its feasible objects inhabit an abstract algebra and no finite optimisation formulation or solver is supplied. |
| distributed_systems | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | Orthogonal central trace partitions are algebraic decompositions; they specify neither communicating processes nor message, failure, or consensus semantics. |
| other (Operator-algebra structural analysis) | True | strong | adaptable | inferred | The paper constructs central projections that split coefficient traces uniformly. These constructions support structural arguments about uniform property Gamma and Jiang–Su absorption, with applicability confined to the stated algebra and trace hypotheses. |

For every finite $K\\subseteq\\mathcal U(A)$ and $\\varepsilon>0$ there is a projection $P\\in D$ such that, for every $x\\in K$, $$\\sup_{\\tau\\in T(D)}\\|[P,x]\\|_{2,\\tau}<\\varepsilon,\n \\qquad\n \\sup_{\\tau\\in T(D)}|\\tau(Px)-\\tfrac12\\tau(x)|<\\varepsilon.$$",
```json
          "source_object": "stmt-r-thm-finite"
        }
      ],
      "space_complexity": null,
      "strength": "none",
      "time_complexity": null,

    },
```

<a id="pm-s6"></a>
## 6. Applicability limits and search

- **limit-completion-route-hypotheses** (hypothesis): The completion route requires the stated unital, separable, simple, infinite-dimensional, nuclear, stably finite algebra with nonempty traces and real rank zero of its completion ultrapower in the quotient norm. Blocked use: ['Apply the completion theorem after checking nuclearity alone.', 'Replace real rank zero of the completion ultrapower by an unproved approximation property of the original algebra.']
- **limit-finite-splitting-small-full-projections** (hypothesis): Finite-set central splitting requires a unital nuclear inclusion, ambient real rank zero, nonempty ambient traces, and full projections of arbitrarily small uniform trace. Simplicity and nuclearity of the ambient algebra are not required. Blocked use: ['Invoke finite-set splitting without establishing the small-full-projection hypothesis.', 'Add ambient simplicity or ambient nuclearity as necessary hypotheses of the finite-set theorem.']
- **limit-finite-tests-remain-approximate** (approximation): The finite-set theorem controls only the prescribed unitary tests at the prescribed tolerance. Exact centrality over the completion requires the subsequent bounded coordinate selection and uniform tracial density argument. Blocked use: ['Treat a finite-test witness as an exact central projection over the entire completion.', 'Omit the passage from the original algebra to its completion.']
- **limit-coefficient-traces-essential** (formal_scope): Uniform property Gamma requires central projections preserving coefficient traces. Equal scalar traces of the projections alone do not establish the property. Blocked use: ['Conclude uniform property Gamma from scalar trace divisibility.', 'Substitute scalar moment tests for all coefficient trace identities.']
- **limit-nuclearity-alone-insufficient** (counterexample_scope): The paper explicitly identifies a simple separable unital nuclear example without uniform property Gamma; nuclearity alone cannot replace the additional hypotheses of the routes. Blocked use: ['Infer uniform property Gamma solely from nuclearity.', 'Read the nuclear averaging argument as an unconditional regularity theorem.']
- **limit-fixed-ultrafilter-and-bounded-sequences** (nonconstructive): The main projection outputs belong to quotients formed with a free ultrafilter and bounded representative sequences. A finite computational representation of these outputs is ABSENT. Blocked use: ['Present the final ultrapower projections as general executable outputs.', 'Replace the prescribed ultrafilter with an additional ultrapower without proving the required transfer.']
- **limit-designated-traces-and-abstract-traces** (formal_scope): The designated completion norm and ultrapower limit-trace family are specified separately from arbitrary abstract traces. Identification of all completion traces uses the additional conclusion of the completion-trace corollary under its full hypotheses. Blocked use: ['Assume every abstract completion trace is designated before establishing the corollary.', 'Replace the specified limit-trace family by arbitrary ultrapower traces in statements that only concern designated traces.']
- **limit-projection-tool-standing-regime** (hypothesis): The projection corner, column splitting, proportional splitting and orthogonal insertion arguments operate under the section's standing ambient real rank zero and nonempty trace assumptions. Small positive support in the simple subalgebra becomes a full ambient projection only after the spectral-cut and quotient argument. Blocked use: ['Use the projection tools in an arbitrary ambient algebra without real rank zero.', 'Infer a small full ambient projection directly from a small positive element without the ambient spectral approximation.']
- **limit-averaging-map-selected-before-input** (formal_scope): The nuclear averaging coefficients are chosen before the input projection. The packing proof uses that order to prepare coefficient tests backwards through the rounds. Blocked use: ["Choose averaging coefficients after seeing the residual projection while retaining the proof's earlier commutator estimates.", 'Omit the coefficient tests needed to control overlap with previously inserted projections.']
- **limit-weighted-blocks-not-orthogonal** (edge_case): The weighted block family may be empty, its base projections may repeat, and its image projections need not be mutually orthogonal. Sampling uses scalar matrix compression rather than centrality of the sampled base subprojection. Blocked use: ['Apply orthogonal-sum energy estimates directly to the weighted image projections.', 'Require or assume that every sampled base subprojection commutes with the compressed coefficients.', 'Replace weighted mass and energy estimates by a bound on the raw number of blocks.']
- **limit-packing-parameter-order** (approximation): The packing argument first fixes the parameter controlling unoccupied mass and then increases the number of rounds with that parameter fixed. The resulting estimates do not give an input-independent bound on the underlying block constructions. Blocked use: ['Vary the mass-exhaustion parameter and the number of rounds without checking the energy and halving error bounds together.', 'Interpret convergence of the error budgets as a uniform computational complexity bound.']
- **limit-unital-comparison-and-empty-traces** (hypothesis): The direct unital absorption theorem assumes simplicity, separability, unitality, infinite dimensionality, nuclearity and strict comparison in the stated convention. When traces are absent, that convention still requires comparison with every nonzero positive target in the stabilization. Blocked use: ['Invoke absorption merely because the tracial-state space is empty.', 'Apply the unital theorem to a nonsimple or finite-dimensional algebra without a separate argument.']
- **limit-rank-rounding-matrix-size** (unknown): The trace-to-rank approximation has no asserted matrix-size bound independent of the positive element and tolerance. It does not require separability, nuclearity or strict comparison, although later hereditary placement does require comparison. Blocked use: ['Assign a uniform matrix-size or running-time budget to rank approximation.', 'Treat the rank approximation alone as a proof that the approximant lies in a prescribed hereditary subalgebra.']
- **limit-hereditary-support-and-bidual-order** (formal_scope): The almost-projection lemma assumes the positive target has norm strictly below the unit bound. Exact projection supply is a conclusion in the tracial ultrapower, with its spectral order bound interpreted in the bidual and its cutoff relation holding in the quotient itself. Blocked use: ['Assert exact projection supply inside the original algebra from the almost-projection lemma.', 'Replace bidual spectral support by an unsupported assertion of commutation with the positive element.', 'Drop the norm restriction in the hereditary almost-projection argument.']
- **limit-centering-is-not-commutation** (formal_scope): Centered symmetries commute with the prescribed finite-dimensional algebra and are trace-centered against the separable coefficient algebra. The proposition does not assert commutation with that coefficient algebra or mutual commutation of successive symmetries. Blocked use: ['Use trace centering as an operator commutation relation.', 'Treat the initial weighted symmetry sum as a sum of independent commuting random variables.']
- **limit-nuclear-approximation-and-observable-norm** (unknown): The nuclear approximation theorem supplies finitely many outgoing order-zero summands for each request without an asserted uniform bound. The averaged observable consequently has no asserted norm bound independent of the number of summands. Blocked use: ['Infer bounded nuclear dimension from the unrestricted convex approximation theorem.', 'Diagonalize the uncut averaged observables in a common norm ball without further estimates.', 'Attach a uniform finite resource budget to homomorphism replacement and symmetry averaging.']
- **limit-clipping-retains-moment-errors** (approximation): At a fixed clipping radius the coefficient moment error terms remain; only the additional local tolerance is arbitrarily small. The radius provides a common norm bound independent of the nuclear approximation. Blocked use: ['Obtain exact first and second coefficient moments at fixed radius by decreasing only the local tolerance.', 'Discard clipping while retaining the fixed-ball diagonal argument.']
- **limit-diagonal-fixed-balls-and-separability** (hypothesis): The tracial diagonal principle requires finitely many variables in fixed finite-radius domains, countably many polynomial tests, and approximate satisfaction of every finite initial collection. Separability is used to pass from dense coefficient families to all coefficients. Blocked use: ['Apply the principle to variables with growing norm bounds.', 'Impose an arbitrary uncountable family of coefficient conditions without a density reduction.', 'Infer exact equations from a single approximate trial tuple.']
- **limit-gaussian-rows-and-fixed-test-functions** (formal_scope): The conditional central limit construction uses coefficient moment bounds against the entire algebra generated by earlier variables. It assumes neither independence nor equality of the variable distributions. Uniform weak convergence is stated for each fixed positive coefficient and fixed bounded continuous function. Blocked use: ['Replace conditional coefficient tests by scalar moments alone.', 'Claim independence or identical distribution of the row variables.', 'Apply the weak convergence lemma directly to discontinuous interval indicators or varying test functions without the continuous approximation argument.']
- **limit-gaussian-sums-not-diagonal-variables** (nonconstructive): The normalized Gaussian sums are used separately rather than as a bounded representative sequence. Exact central partitions are obtained by diagonalizing bounded positive contractions formed from continuous partition functions. Blocked use: ['Use the normalized sums themselves as fixed-ball diagonal variables.', 'Claim exact projections before controlling the approximate projection defects and the sum-to-unit equation.']
- **limit-projectionless-trace-and-comparison-hypotheses** (hypothesis): The nonunital route assumes a nonzero simple separable nuclear stably projectionless algebra, boundedness of every densely finite lower-semicontinuous trace, a nonempty compact normalized trace base, and the displayed comparison on traces normalized at finite positive target rank. Blocked use: ['Extend the theorem to unbounded densely finite traces.', 'Omit compactness or nonemptiness of the normalized trace base.', 'Replace the comparison hypothesis by a test on only a selected subset of normalized traces.', 'Assume all stabilized ranks are finite.']
- **limit-nonunital-unit-and-supported-selection** (hypothesis): Compactness of the normalized trace base is used to obtain a unit in the tracial quotient. Supported projection selection requires positive contractions satisfying the exact support relation and trace domination at every designated limit trace; its proof uses finite-target-rank comparison and an auxiliary matrix buffer. Blocked use: ["Assume a nonunital algebra's tracial quotient is automatically unital without the uniform approximate-unit argument.", 'Select a prescribed projection trace without establishing support and trace domination.', 'Remove the comparison buffer while retaining the strict rank inequality.']
- **limit-cost-selection-not-optimisation** (formal_scope): Cost selection supplies a prescribed trace proportion and a one-sided bound against a positive observable. Repetition along matrix units supplies commutation with a finite-dimensional unital algebra; an optimal objective value or finite solver is ABSENT. Blocked use: ['Interpret the selected projection as a cost minimizer.', 'Apply the positive-cost bound to an arbitrary signed observable without an additional argument.', 'Drop finite dimensionality or unitality in the commutant-cost corollary.']
- **limit-models-joint-cost-and-truncation** (approximation): The finite-dimensional models control total weighted squared tracial error. The small-splitting proof combines overlap and model error in a single positive cost before truncating the projection sum. Truncation controls tracial commutators rather than operator-norm commutators. Blocked use: ['Replace weighted average approximation by a uniform approximation assertion for every individual model.', 'Control only overlap while ignoring model error in the selected cost.', 'Treat the sum of selected projections as a contraction before truncation.', 'Promote the tracial Lipschitz estimate to an operator-norm Lipschitz estimate.']
- **limit-small-splittings-need-amplification** (approximation): The local and relative small splittings are positive contractions with retained trace and projection errors at fixed proportion. Exact relative projections require successively commuting complements, vanishing accumulated errors and a bounded diagonal passage. Blocked use: ['Treat a small splitting as an exact projection.', 'Remove the retained proportion-dependent error merely by decreasing the local tolerance.', 'Multiply complements without first arranging their commutation.']
- **limit-distinct-central-sequence-quotients** (formal_scope): The uniform tracial quotient, norm ultrapower and annihilator quotient have distinct roles. The nonunital absorption passage requires central lifting, containment of the annihilator in the trace-kernel ideal, and tracial largeness of the induced order-zero maps. Blocked use: ['Identify the norm ultrapower with the uniform tracial quotient.', 'Assume a central tracial element has a norm-central lift without the lifting argument.', 'Conclude nonunital absorption from scalar quotient trace identities alone.']
- **limit-generalized-traces-finiteness-test** (hypothesis): The generalized trace normalization lemma concerns traces finite at a fixed nonzero positive element. Its proof uses boundedness of densely finite coordinate traces and compactness of the normalized base to bound their scalar normalizations. Blocked use: ['Apply the normalization conclusion to a generalized trace infinite on every nonzero positive element.', 'Remove the finite nonzero test element or the compact-base bound from the normalization argument.', 'Ignore the Pedersen-ideal largeness tests in the final absorption interface.']

### Formal statement (Lean 4)

Corpus status: formalized in the source corpus (toolchain `leanprover/lean4:v4.34.1`, Mathlib `d13f23b723b8a846827a245b89c10fc7d3f11612`). Not re-checked here; the statements give the exact quantifiers and hypotheses, not a proof.

Scope note of family 291 (`lean/docs/291.md`, verbatim):

> The following describes the scope of the Lean formalization related to the following accompanying paper(s):
>
> - [Tracial projection methods and uniform property $\Gamma$](../../preprints/Tracial-projection-methods-and-uniform-property-Gamma-September-23-2026/paper.pdf)
>
> ## Scope
>
> The formalization proves that real rank zero of the uniform tracial ultrapower of the uniform tracial completion implies uniform property $\Gamma$ for a simple separable unital infinite-dimensional nuclear stably finite $C^*$-algebra with traces. The conclusion holds at every specified free ultrafilter under the corresponding real-rank-zero hypothesis.
>
> The linked comparison results also prove Jiang–Su absorption and uniform property $\Gamma$ for simple separable unital infinite-dimensional nuclear algebras with strict comparison, and for the stated stably projectionless nuclear algebras with bounded densely finite traces, a nonempty compact normalized trace base, and the prescribed finite-target-rank comparison condition.

```lean
import Mathlib

namespace OAI

noncomputable section

/-!
The final claim constructs the tracial quotients and states the original gamma assertion.
`Analysis.TracialSplitting.Challenge.main_iff_original` verifies statement preservation.
-/

universe uA uD uE uI uR uX u v

namespace Pureness

open scoped ComplexOrder

structure CStarAlgebraNorm (R : Type uR) [Ring R] [StarRing R] [Algebra ℂ R]
    extends AlgebraNorm ℂ R where
  cstar : ∀ x, toAlgebraNorm (star x * x) = toAlgebraNorm x * toAlgebraNorm x

structure TracialState (A : Type uA) [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] where
  functional : A →ₚ[ℂ] ℂ
  normalized : functional 1 = 1
  tracial : ∀ a b : A, functional (a * b) = functional (b * a)

structure ClosedStarIdeal (D : Type uD) [CStarAlgebra D] where
  ideal : TwoSidedIdeal D
  star_mem : ∀ {u : D}, u ∈ ideal → star u ∈ ideal
  isClosed : IsClosed (ideal : Set D)

end Pureness

universe uNuclear

namespace ComparatorModel

namespace Pureness

open scoped TensorProduct ComplexOrder

abbrev CStarAlgebraNorm := @_root_.OAI.Pureness.CStarAlgebraNorm

def IsNuclear (A : Type u) [CStarAlgebra A] : Prop :=
  ∀ (B : Type v) [CStarAlgebra B], Subsingleton (CStarAlgebraNorm (A ⊗[ℂ] B))

abbrev TracialState := @_root_.OAI.Pureness.TracialState

end Pureness

namespace CurrentMain

open _root_.OAI.ComparatorModel.Pureness

open scoped ComplexOrder

def RealRankZero (D : Type) [CStarAlgebra D] : Prop :=
  ∀ a : D, IsSelfAdjoint a → ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε →
    ∃ b : D, IsSelfAdjoint b ∧ (spectrum ℂ b).Finite ∧ ‖a - b‖ < ε

def traceTwoNorm {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D]
    (τ : TracialState D) (a : D) : ℝ :=
  Real.sqrt (τ.functional (star a * a)).re

end CurrentMain

section

open Filter

open scoped Topology NNReal ENNReal

abbrev Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal := @_root_.OAI.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal

namespace Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal

section

variable {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] (J : ClosedStarIdeal D)

abbrev Quotient := J.ideal.ringCon.Quotient

def quotientMap : D →ₐ[ℂ] J.Quotient := J.ideal.ringCon.mkₐ ℂ

instance quotientStar : Star (J.Quotient) where
  star := Quotient.map' star (by
    intro u v h
    change J.ideal.ringCon u v at h
    change J.ideal.ringCon (star u) (star v)
    rw [TwoSidedIdeal.rel_iff] at h ⊢
    simpa only [star_sub] using J.star_mem h)

instance quotientStarRing : StarRing (J.Quotient) where
  star_involutive x := Quotient.inductionOn' x (fun u => congrArg J.quotientMap (star_star u))
  star_mul x y := Quotient.inductionOn₂' x y (fun u v => congrArg J.quotientMap (star_mul u v))
  star_add x y := Quotient.inductionOn₂' x y (fun u v => congrArg J.quotientMap (star_add u v))

instance quotientStarModule : StarModule ℂ (J.Quotient) where
  star_smul c x := Quotient.inductionOn' x (fun u => congrArg J.quotientMap (star_smul c u))

abbrev idealSubmodule : Submodule ℂ (D) :=
  (J.quotientMap).toLinearMap.ker

instance idealSubmodule_closed : IsClosed (J.idealSubmodule : Set D) := by
  have he : (J.idealSubmodule : Set D) = (J.ideal : Set D) := by
    ext u
    change J.ideal.ringCon.mk' u = J.ideal.ringCon.mk' 0 ↔ u ∈ J.ideal
    exact Quotient.eq
  rw [he]
  exact J.isClosed

def quotientLinearEquiv :
    (D ⧸ J.idealSubmodule) ≃ₗ[ℂ] J.Quotient :=
  (J.quotientMap).toLinearMap.quotKerEquivOfSurjective ((J.ideal.ringCon.mkₐ_surjective (α := ℂ)))

instance quotientNormedAddCommGroup : NormedAddCommGroup (J.Quotient) :=
  NormedAddCommGroup.induced _ _ (J.quotientLinearEquiv).symm
    (J.quotientLinearEquiv).symm.injective

instance quotientNormedSpace : NormedSpace ℂ (J.Quotient) :=
  NormedSpace.induced ℂ _ _ (J.quotientLinearEquiv).symm

def quotientNormEquiv : J.Quotient ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ]
    (D ⧸ J.idealSubmodule) where
  __ := (J.quotientLinearEquiv).symm
  norm_map' _ := rfl

instance quotientCompleteSpace : CompleteSpace (J.Quotient) :=
  (J.quotientNormEquiv).toIsometryEquiv.completeSpace_iff.mpr inferInstance

class NormConstruction : Prop where
  mul_le : ∀ {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] (J : ClosedStarIdeal D) (x y : J.Quotient),
    ‖x*y‖ ≤ ‖x‖*‖y‖
  star_eq : ∀ {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] (J : ClosedStarIdeal D) (x : J.Quotient),
    ‖star x‖ = ‖x‖
  square_le : ∀ {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]
    (J : ClosedStarIdeal D) (x : J.Quotient), ‖x‖ * ‖x‖ ≤ ‖star x * x‖

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction]

instance quotientNormedRing : NormedRing (J.Quotient) where
  dist_eq := dist_eq_norm_neg_add
  norm_mul_le := NormConstruction.mul_le J

instance quotientNormedAlgebra : NormedAlgebra ℂ (J.Quotient) where
  norm_smul_le := norm_smul_le

instance quotientNormedStarGroup : NormedStarGroup (J.Quotient) where
  norm_star_le x := (NormConstruction.star_eq J x).le

end

open Filter

open scoped Topology NNReal ENNReal

variable {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]

variable (J : ClosedStarIdeal D)

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction]

instance quotientCStarAlgebra : CStarAlgebra (J.Quotient) where
  norm_mul_self_le := NormConstruction.square_le J

end Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal

end

section

open _root_.OAI.ComparatorModel.Pureness

section UniformTraceNorm

variable {D : Type} {I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]

variable (τ : I → TracialState D) [Nonempty I]

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction]

def CurrentMain.familyTwoNorm (a : D) : ℝ := ⨆ i, traceTwoNorm (τ i) a

end UniformTraceNorm

open _root_.OAI.ComparatorModel.Pureness Filter

open scoped Topology NNReal ENNReal

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction]

instance CurrentMain.boundedFamilyProductCStar {X : Type} {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [Nontrivial D] :
    CStarAlgebra (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) where

section UniformNull

variable {D : Type} {I : Type} {X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]

variable (τ : I → TracialState D) [Nonempty I]

def CurrentMain.IsFamilyNull (F : Filter X) (u : X → D) : Prop :=
  Tendsto (fun n => familyTwoNorm τ (u n)) F (𝓝 0)

class CurrentMain.NullConstruction : Prop where
  zero : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]
    (F : Filter X), IsFamilyNull τ F (fun _ => 0)
  add : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]
    {F : Filter X} {u v : X → D}, IsFamilyNull τ F u → IsFamilyNull τ F v →
    IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n + v n)
  neg : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]
    {F : Filter X} {u : X → D}, IsFamilyNull τ F u → IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => -u n)
  star : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]
    {F : Filter X} {u : X → D}, IsFamilyNull τ F u → IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => star (u n))
  mul_left : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]
    {F : Filter X} {u v : X → D}, IsFamilyNull τ F v → ∀ {M : ℝ},
    (∀ n, ‖u n‖ ≤ M) → IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n * v n)
  mul_right : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]
    {F : Filter X} {u v : X → D}, IsFamilyNull τ F u → ∀ {M : ℝ},
    (∀ n, ‖v n‖ ≤ M) → IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n * v n)
  closed : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] (F : Filter X),
    IsClosed {u : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞ | IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n)}

variable [Nontrivial D]

variable [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.NullConstruction]

def CurrentMain.familyNullTwoSided (F : Filter X) : TwoSidedIdeal (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) :=
  TwoSidedIdeal.mk' {u | IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n)}
    (NullConstruction.zero τ F)
    (fun hu hv => NullConstruction.add τ hu hv)
    (fun hu => NullConstruction.neg τ hu)
    (fun {u _} hv => NullConstruction.mul_left τ hv
      (lp.norm_apply_le_norm ENNReal.top_ne_zero u))
    (fun {_ v} hu => NullConstruction.mul_right τ hu
      (lp.norm_apply_le_norm ENNReal.top_ne_zero v))

def CurrentMain.familyNullIdeal (F : Filter X) : ClosedStarIdeal (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) where
  ideal := familyNullTwoSided τ F
  star_mem := by
    intro u hu
    apply (TwoSidedIdeal.mem_mk' _ _ _ _ _ _ (star u)).mpr
    exact NullConstruction.star τ ((TwoSidedIdeal.mem_mk' _ _ _ _ _ _ u).mp hu)
  isClosed := by
    have he : (familyNullTwoSided τ F : Set (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞)) =
        {u | IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n)} := by
      ext u
      exact TwoSidedIdeal.mem_mk' _ _ _ _ _ _ u
    rw [he]
    exact NullConstruction.closed τ F

end UniformNull

variable {D : Type} {I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]

variable (τ : I → TracialState D) [Nonempty I]

variable [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.NullConstruction]

def CurrentMain.IsFamilyCauchy (u : ℕ → D) : Prop :=
  IsFamilyNull τ (atTop : Filter (ℕ × ℕ)) (fun p => u p.1 - u p.2)

class CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction : Prop where
  const : ∀ {D I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]
    (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] (a : D), IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun _ => a)
  add : ∀ {D I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]
    (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] {u v : ℕ → D},
    IsFamilyCauchy τ u → IsFamilyCauchy τ v → IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun n => u n+v n)
  star : ∀ {D I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]
    (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] {u : ℕ → D},
    IsFamilyCauchy τ u → IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun n => star (u n))
  mul : ∀ {D I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]
    (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] {u v : ℕ → D},
    IsFamilyCauchy τ u → IsFamilyCauchy τ v → ∀ {M N : ℝ},
    (∀ n, ‖u n‖ ≤ M) → (∀ n, ‖v n‖ ≤ N) → IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun n => u n*v n)
  closed : ∀ {D I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]
    (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] [Nontrivial D],
    IsClosed {u : lp (fun _ : ℕ => D) ∞ | IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun n => u n)}

variable [Nontrivial D]

variable [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction]

def CurrentMain.familyCauchyAlgebra : StarSubalgebra ℂ (lp (fun _ : ℕ => D) ∞) where
  carrier := {u | IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun n => u n)}
  zero_mem' := CauchyConstruction.const τ 0
  one_mem' := CauchyConstruction.const τ 1
  add_mem' hu hv := CauchyConstruction.add τ hu hv
  mul_mem' {u v} hu hv := CauchyConstruction.mul τ hu hv
    (lp.norm_apply_le_norm ENNReal.top_ne_zero u)
    (lp.norm_apply_le_norm ENNReal.top_ne_zero v)
  algebraMap_mem' z := CauchyConstruction.const τ (algebraMap ℂ D z)
  star_mem' hu := CauchyConstruction.star τ hu

instance CurrentMain.familyCauchy_complete : CompleteSpace (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) :=
  (CauchyConstruction.closed τ).completeSpace_coe

instance CurrentMain.familyCauchy_cstar : CStarAlgebra (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) where

def CurrentMain.familyCauchyNullIdeal : ClosedStarIdeal (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) where
  ideal := TwoSidedIdeal.comap (familyCauchyAlgebra τ).val.toRingHom
    (familyNullIdeal τ atTop).ideal
  star_mem := by
    intro u hu
    apply (familyNullIdeal τ atTop).star_mem hu
  isClosed := by
    exact (familyNullIdeal τ atTop).isClosed.preimage
      (continuous_subtype_val : Continuous (fun u : familyCauchyAlgebra τ => u.1))

abbrev CurrentMain.UniformTracialCompletion := (familyCauchyNullIdeal τ).Quotient

end

section

open scoped ComplexOrder

variable {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.NullConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction]

instance Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.quotientOrder (J : ClosedStarIdeal D) : PartialOrder J.Quotient :=
  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder _

instance Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.quotientStarOrdered (J : ClosedStarIdeal D) : StarOrderedRing J.Quotient :=
  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing _

variable (J : Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal D)

variable (τ : Pureness.TracialState D) (hτ : ∀ a ∈ J.ideal, τ.functional a = 0)

def Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.descendedValue : J.Quotient → ℂ := Quotient.lift τ.functional (by
  intro u v huv
  change J.ideal.ringCon u v at huv
  rw [TwoSidedIdeal.rel_iff] at huv
  have h := hτ (u-v) huv
  rw [map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h
  exact h)

def Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.descendedLinear : J.Quotient →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ where
  toFun := J.descendedValue τ hτ
  map_add' x y := Quotient.inductionOn₂' x y (fun u v => τ.functional.map_add u v)
  map_smul' c x := Quotient.inductionOn' x (fun u => τ.functional.map_smul c u)

def Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.descendedTrace : TracialState J.Quotient where
  functional := { J.descendedLinear τ hτ with
    monotone' := by
      intro x y hxy
      apply sub_nonneg.mp
      change 0 ≤ J.descendedLinear τ hτ y - J.descendedLinear τ hτ x
      rw [← map_sub]
      obtain ⟨z,hz⟩ := CStarAlgebra.nonneg_iff_eq_star_mul_self.mp (sub_nonneg.mpr hxy)
      rw [hz]
      induction z using Quotient.inductionOn' with
      | h u => exact τ.functional.map_nonneg (star_mul_self_nonneg u) }
  normalized := τ.normalized
  tracial x y := Quotient.inductionOn₂' x y (fun u v => τ.tracial u v)

end

section

open _root_.OAI.ComparatorModel.Pureness Filter

open scoped Topology NNReal ENNReal ComplexOrder

section UltraTrace

variable {D : Type} {X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] [Nontrivial D]

variable (θ : X → TracialState D) (U : Ultrafilter X)

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.NullConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction]

instance CurrentMain.boundedFamilyProductOrder : PartialOrder (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) :=
  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder _

instance CurrentMain.boundedFamilyProductStarOrdered : StarOrderedRing (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) :=
  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing _

def CurrentMain.ultraTraceValue (u : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) : ℂ :=
  limUnder (U : Filter X) (fun n => (θ n).functional (u n))

class CurrentMain.LimitConstruction : Prop where
  tendsto : ∀ {D X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]
    (θ : X → Pureness.TracialState D) (U : Ultrafilter X) (u : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞),
    Tendsto (fun n => (θ n).functional (u n)) (U : Filter X) (𝓝 (ultraTraceValue θ U u))

variable [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.LimitConstruction]

def CurrentMain.ultraTraceLinear : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞ →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ where
  toFun := ultraTraceValue θ U
  map_add' u v := by
    apply tendsto_nhds_unique (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U (u+v))
    simpa only [lp.coeFn_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add] using
      (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U u).add (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U v)
  map_smul' z u := by
    apply tendsto_nhds_unique (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U (z • u))
    simpa only [lp.coeFn_smul, Pi.smul_apply, map_smul, RingHom.id_apply, smul_eq_mul] using
      (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U u).const_mul z

def CurrentMain.boundedFamilyEval (n : X) : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞ →⋆ₐ[ℂ] D where
  toFun u := u n
  map_zero' := rfl
  map_one' := rfl
  map_add' _ _ := rfl
  map_mul' _ _ := rfl
  commutes' _ := rfl
  map_star' _ := rfl

def CurrentMain.ultraTrace : TracialState (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) where
  functional := { ultraTraceLinear θ U with
    monotone' := by
      intro u v huv
      exact le_of_tendsto_of_tendsto (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U u)
        (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U v) (Eventually.of_forall (fun n =>
          (θ n).functional.monotone (map_rel (boundedFamilyEval n) huv))) }
  normalized := by
    apply tendsto_nhds_unique (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U 1)
    have he (n : X) : (θ n).functional ((1 : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) n) = 1 :=
      (θ n).normalized
    simp only [he]
    exact tendsto_const_nhds
  tracial u v := by
    change ultraTraceValue θ U (u*v) = ultraTraceValue θ U (v*u)
    apply tendsto_nhds_unique (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U (u*v))
    have he (n : X) : (θ n).functional ((u*v) n) = (θ n).functional ((v*u) n) :=
      (θ n).tracial (u n) (v n)
    simpa only [he] using LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U (v*u)

end UltraTrace

section UniformQuotient

variable {D : Type} {I : Type} {X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] [Nontrivial D]

variable (τ : I → TracialState D) [Nonempty I] (U : Ultrafilter X)

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.NullConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.LimitConstruction]

abbrev CurrentMain.FamilyUltrapower := (familyNullIdeal τ (U : Filter X)).Quotient

class CurrentMain.TraceConstruction : Prop where
  null : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] [Nontrivial D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]
    (U : Ultrafilter X) (s : X → I) (u : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞),
    IsFamilyNull τ (U : Filter X) (fun n => u n) →
      (ultraTrace (fun n => τ (s n)) U).functional u = 0

variable [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.TraceConstruction]

def CurrentMain.familyLimitTrace (s : X → I) : TracialState (FamilyUltrapower τ U) :=
  (familyNullIdeal τ (U : Filter X)).descendedTrace
    (ultraTrace (fun n => τ (s n)) U) (fun u hu => by
      apply TraceConstruction.null τ U s u
      have he : u ∈ familyNullTwoSided τ (U : Filter X) ↔
          IsFamilyNull τ (U : Filter X) (fun n => u n) := by
        unfold familyNullTwoSided
        exact TwoSidedIdeal.mem_mk' _ _ _ _ _ _ u
      exact he.mp hu)

end UniformQuotient

section PullbackTrace

variable {D : Type} {E : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]

variable [CStarAlgebra E] [PartialOrder E] [StarOrderedRing E]

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.NullConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.LimitConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.TraceConstruction]

def CurrentMain.pullbackTrace (τ : TracialState E) (ι : D →⋆ₐ[ℂ] E) : TracialState D where
  functional := { τ.functional.toLinearMap.comp ι.toLinearMap with
    monotone' := fun _ _ h => τ.functional.monotone (map_rel ι h) }
  normalized := by change τ.functional (ι 1) = 1; rw [map_one, τ.normalized]
  tracial x y := by
    change τ.functional (ι (x*y)) = τ.functional (ι (y*x))
    rw [map_mul ι x y, map_mul ι y x]
    exact τ.tracial _ _

end PullbackTrace

section CompletionTraces

variable {D : Type} {I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]

variable [Nontrivial D] (τ : I → TracialState D) [Nonempty I]

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.NullConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.LimitConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.TraceConstruction]

instance CurrentMain.familyCauchyOrder : PartialOrder (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) :=
  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder _

instance CurrentMain.familyCauchyStarOrdered : StarOrderedRing (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) :=
  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing _

def CurrentMain.cauchyTrace (i : I) : TracialState (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) :=
  pullbackTrace (ultraTrace (fun _ : ℕ => τ i) (Ultrafilter.of atTop))
    (familyCauchyAlgebra τ).subtype

def CurrentMain.completionTrace (i : I) : TracialState (UniformTracialCompletion τ) :=
  (familyCauchyNullIdeal τ).descendedTrace (cauchyTrace τ i) (fun u hu => by
    apply TraceConstruction.null τ (Ultrafilter.of atTop) (fun _ => i) u.1
    have he : u.1 ∈ familyNullTwoSided τ atTop ↔
        IsFamilyNull τ atTop (fun n => u.1 n) := by
      unfold familyNullTwoSided
      exact TwoSidedIdeal.mem_mk' _ _ _ _ _ _ u.1
    have hn : IsFamilyNull τ atTop (fun n => u.1 n) := he.mp hu
    exact hn.mono_left (Ultrafilter.of_le atTop))

def CurrentMain.boundedFamilyConstant {X : Type} (a : D) : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞ :=
  ⟨fun _ => a, memℓp_infty_iff.mpr ⟨‖a‖,by rintro _ ⟨n,rfl⟩; exact le_rfl⟩⟩

instance CurrentMain.completionNontrivial : Nontrivial (UniformTracialCompletion τ) := by
  obtain ⟨i⟩ := ‹Nonempty I›
  refine ⟨⟨1,0,?_⟩⟩
  intro h
  have he := congrArg (completionTrace τ i).functional h
  rw [(completionTrace τ i).normalized, map_zero] at he
  exact one_ne_zero he

end CompletionTraces

variable {D : Type} {I : Type} {X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]

variable [Nontrivial D] (τ : I → TracialState D) [Nonempty I] (U : Ultrafilter X)

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.NullConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.LimitConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.TraceConstruction]

def CurrentMain.familyUltrapowerMap : D →⋆ₐ[ℂ] FamilyUltrapower τ U where
  toFun a := (familyNullIdeal τ (U : Filter X)).quotientMap (boundedFamilyConstant a)
  map_zero' := (familyNullIdeal τ (U : Filter X)).quotientMap.map_zero
  map_one' := (familyNullIdeal τ (U : Filter X)).quotientMap.map_one
  map_add' a b := (familyNullIdeal τ (U : Filter X)).quotientMap.map_add
    (boundedFamilyConstant a) (boundedFamilyConstant b)
  map_mul' a b := (familyNullIdeal τ (U : Filter X)).quotientMap.map_mul
    (boundedFamilyConstant a) (boundedFamilyConstant b)
  commutes' z := (familyNullIdeal τ (U : Filter X)).quotientMap.commutes z
  map_star' _a := rfl

end

section

open Filter Topology

variable {A : Type uA} [NormedRing A]

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.NullConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.LimitConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.TraceConstruction]

def Pureness.TopologicallySimple (A : Type uA) [NormedRing A] : Prop :=
  ∀ I : TwoSidedIdeal A, IsClosed (I : Set A) → I = ⊥ ∨ I = ⊤

end

section

open _root_.OAI.ComparatorModel.Pureness Filter

variable [ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.NullConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.LimitConstruction] [ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.TraceConstruction]

def CurrentMain.StablyFinite (A : Type uA) [CStarAlgebra A] : Prop :=
  ∀ n : ℕ, ∀ v : CStarMatrix (Fin n) (Fin n) A,
    star v*v = 1 → v*star v = 1

def CurrentMain.UniformPropertyGammaAt (A : Type) [CStarAlgebra A]
    [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A] [Nontrivial A]
    [Nonempty (TracialState A)] (U : Ultrafilter ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ p : FamilyUltrapower (completionTrace (fun τ : TracialState A => τ)) U,
    IsStarProjection p ∧ ∀ x : UniformTracialCompletion (fun τ : TracialState A => τ),
      p * familyUltrapowerMap (completionTrace (fun τ : TracialState A => τ)) U x =
        familyUltrapowerMap (completionTrace (fun τ : TracialState A => τ)) U x * p ∧
      ∀ s : ℕ → TracialState A,
        (familyLimitTrace (completionTrace (fun τ : TracialState A => τ)) U s).functional
          (p * familyUltrapowerMap (completionTrace (fun τ : TracialState A => τ)) U x) =
        (1/2 : ℂ) *
        (familyLimitTrace (completionTrace (fun τ : TracialState A => τ)) U s).functional
          (familyUltrapowerMap (completionTrace (fun τ : TracialState A => τ)) U x)

open _root_.OAI.ComparatorModel.Pureness Filter

def CurrentMain.Statement : Prop := ∀
    {A : Type} [CStarAlgebra A] [PartialOrder A] [StarOrderedRing A]
    [Nontrivial A] [TopologicalSpace.SeparableSpace A]
    (_hsimple : TopologicallySimple A) (_hinf : ¬FiniteDimensional ℂ A)
    (_hA : IsNuclear.{0, uNuclear} A) (_hfinite : StablyFinite A)
    [Nonempty (TracialState A)] (U : Ultrafilter ℕ)
    (_hfree : (U : Filter ℕ) ≤ atTop)
    (_hRR : RealRankZero (FamilyUltrapower (completionTrace (fun τ : TracialState A => τ)) U)),
    UniformPropertyGammaAt A U

end

def CurrentMain.MainClaim : Prop :=
  ∃ h0 : ComparatorModel.Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.NormConstruction, letI := h0;
  ∃ h1 : ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.NullConstruction, letI := h1;
  ∃ h2 : ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction, letI := h2;
  ∃ h3 : ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.LimitConstruction, letI := h3;
  ∃ h4 : ComparatorModel.CurrentMain.TraceConstruction, letI := h4;
  Statement.{uNuclear}

theorem CurrentMain.main : MainClaim.{uNuclear} := by
  sorry

end ComparatorModel

end

end OAI
```

```json
      "statement_lean": "instance quotientStar : Star (J.Quotient) where\n  star := Quotient.map' star (by\n    intro u v h\n    change J.ideal.ringCon u v at h\n    change J.ideal.ringCon (star u) (star v)\n    rw [TwoSidedIdeal.rel_iff] at h ⊢\n    simpa only [star_sub] using J.star_mem h)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "quotientStarRing",
      "statement_lean": "instance quotientStarRing : StarRing (J.Quotient) where\n  star_involutive x := Quotient.inductionOn' x (fun u => congrArg J.quotientMap (star_star u))\n  star_mul x y := Quotient.inductionOn₂' x y (fun u v => congrArg J.quotientMap (star_mul u v))\n  star_add x y := Quotient.inductionOn₂' x y (fun u v => congrArg J.quotientMap (star_add u v))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "quotientStarModule",
      "statement_lean": "instance quotientStarModule : StarModule ℂ (J.Quotient) where\n  star_smul c x := Quotient.inductionOn' x (fun u => congrArg J.quotientMap (star_smul c u))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "abbrev",
      "name": "idealSubmodule",
      "statement_lean": "abbrev idealSubmodule : Submodule ℂ (D) :=\n  (J.quotientMap).toLinearMap.ker"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "idealSubmodule_closed",
      "statement_lean": "instance idealSubmodule_closed : IsClosed (J.idealSubmodule : Set D) := by\n  have he : (J.idealSubmodule : Set D) = (J.ideal : Set D) := by\n    ext u\n    change J.ideal.ringCon.mk' u = J.ideal.ringCon.mk' 0 ↔ u ∈ J.ideal\n    exact Quotient.eq\n  rw [he]\n  exact J.isClosed"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "quotientLinearEquiv",
      "statement_lean": "def quotientLinearEquiv :\n    (D ⧸ J.idealSubmodule) ≃ₗ[ℂ] J.Quotient :=\n  (J.quotientMap).toLinearMap.quotKerEquivOfSurjective ((J.ideal.ringCon.mkₐ_surjective (α := ℂ)))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "quotientNormedAddCommGroup",
      "statement_lean": "instance quotientNormedAddCommGroup : NormedAddCommGroup (J.Quotient) :=\n  NormedAddCommGroup.induced _ _ (J.quotientLinearEquiv).symm\n    (J.quotientLinearEquiv).symm.injective"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "quotientNormedSpace",
      "statement_lean": "instance quotientNormedSpace : NormedSpace ℂ (J.Quotient) :=\n  NormedSpace.induced ℂ _ _ (J.quotientLinearEquiv).symm"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "quotientNormEquiv",
      "statement_lean": "def quotientNormEquiv : J.Quotient ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ]\n    (D ⧸ J.idealSubmodule) where\n  __ := (J.quotientLinearEquiv).symm\n  norm_map' _ := rfl"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "quotientCompleteSpace",
      "statement_lean": "instance quotientCompleteSpace : CompleteSpace (J.Quotient) :=\n  (J.quotientNormEquiv).toIsometryEquiv.completeSpace_iff.mpr inferInstance"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "class",
      "name": "NormConstruction",
      "statement_lean": "class NormConstruction : Prop where\n  mul_le : ∀ {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] (J : ClosedStarIdeal D) (x y : J.Quotient),\n    ‖x*y‖ ≤ ‖x‖*‖y‖\n  star_eq : ∀ {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] (J : ClosedStarIdeal D) (x : J.Quotient),\n    ‖star x‖ = ‖x‖\n  square_le : ∀ {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]\n    (J : ClosedStarIdeal D) (x : J.Quotient), ‖x‖ * ‖x‖ ≤ ‖star x * x‖"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "quotientNormedRing",
      "statement_lean": "instance quotientNormedRing : NormedRing (J.Quotient) where\n  dist_eq := dist_eq_norm_neg_add\n  norm_mul_le := NormConstruction.mul_le J"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "quotientNormedAlgebra",
      "statement_lean": "instance quotientNormedAlgebra : NormedAlgebra ℂ (J.Quotient) where\n  norm_smul_le := norm_smul_le"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "quotientNormedStarGroup",
      "statement_lean": "instance quotientNormedStarGroup : NormedStarGroup (J.Quotient) where\n  norm_star_le x := (NormConstruction.star_eq J x).le"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "quotientCStarAlgebra",
      "statement_lean": "instance quotientCStarAlgebra : CStarAlgebra (J.Quotient) where\n  norm_mul_self_le := NormConstruction.square_le J"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.familyTwoNorm",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.familyTwoNorm (a : D) : ℝ := ⨆ i, traceTwoNorm (τ i) a"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "CurrentMain.boundedFamilyProductCStar",
      "statement_lean": "instance CurrentMain.boundedFamilyProductCStar {X : Type} {D : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [Nontrivial D] :\n    CStarAlgebra (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) where"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.IsFamilyNull",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.IsFamilyNull (F : Filter X) (u : X → D) : Prop :=\n  Tendsto (fun n => familyTwoNorm τ (u n)) F (𝓝 0)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "class",
      "name": "CurrentMain.NullConstruction",
      "statement_lean": "class CurrentMain.NullConstruction : Prop where\n  zero : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]\n    (F : Filter X), IsFamilyNull τ F (fun _ => 0)\n  add : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]\n    {F : Filter X} {u v : X → D}, IsFamilyNull τ F u → IsFamilyNull τ F v →\n    IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n + v n)\n  neg : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]\n    {F : Filter X} {u : X → D}, IsFamilyNull τ F u → IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => -u n)\n  star : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]\n    {F : Filter X} {u : X → D}, IsFamilyNull τ F u → IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => star (u n))\n  mul_left : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]\n    {F : Filter X} {u v : X → D}, IsFamilyNull τ F v → ∀ {M : ℝ},\n    (∀ n, ‖u n‖ ≤ M) → IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n * v n)\n  mul_right : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]\n    {F : Filter X} {u v : X → D}, IsFamilyNull τ F u → ∀ {M : ℝ},\n    (∀ n, ‖v n‖ ≤ M) → IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n * v n)\n  closed : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] (F : Filter X),\n    IsClosed {u : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞ | IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n)}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.familyNullTwoSided",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.familyNullTwoSided (F : Filter X) : TwoSidedIdeal (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) :=\n  TwoSidedIdeal.mk' {u | IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n)}\n    (NullConstruction.zero τ F)\n    (fun hu hv => NullConstruction.add τ hu hv)\n    (fun hu => NullConstruction.neg τ hu)\n    (fun {u _} hv => NullConstruction.mul_left τ hv\n      (lp.norm_apply_le_norm ENNReal.top_ne_zero u))\n    (fun {_ v} hu => NullConstruction.mul_right τ hu\n      (lp.norm_apply_le_norm ENNReal.top_ne_zero v))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.familyNullIdeal",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.familyNullIdeal (F : Filter X) : ClosedStarIdeal (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) where\n  ideal := familyNullTwoSided τ F\n  star_mem := by\n    intro u hu\n    apply (TwoSidedIdeal.mem_mk' _ _ _ _ _ _ (star u)).mpr\n    exact NullConstruction.star τ ((TwoSidedIdeal.mem_mk' _ _ _ _ _ _ u).mp hu)\n  isClosed := by\n    have he : (familyNullTwoSided τ F : Set (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞)) =\n        {u | IsFamilyNull τ F (fun n => u n)} := by\n      ext u\n      exact TwoSidedIdeal.mem_mk' _ _ _ _ _ _ u\n    rw [he]\n    exact NullConstruction.closed τ F"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.IsFamilyCauchy",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.IsFamilyCauchy (u : ℕ → D) : Prop :=\n  IsFamilyNull τ (atTop : Filter (ℕ × ℕ)) (fun p => u p.1 - u p.2)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "class",
      "name": "CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction",
      "statement_lean": "class CurrentMain.CauchyConstruction : Prop where\n  const : ∀ {D I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]\n    (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] (a : D), IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun _ => a)\n  add : ∀ {D I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]\n    (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] {u v : ℕ → D},\n    IsFamilyCauchy τ u → IsFamilyCauchy τ v → IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun n => u n+v n)\n  star : ∀ {D I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]\n    (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] {u : ℕ → D},\n    IsFamilyCauchy τ u → IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun n => star (u n))\n  mul : ∀ {D I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]\n    (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] {u v : ℕ → D},\n    IsFamilyCauchy τ u → IsFamilyCauchy τ v → ∀ {M N : ℝ},\n    (∀ n, ‖u n‖ ≤ M) → (∀ n, ‖v n‖ ≤ N) → IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun n => u n*v n)\n  closed : ∀ {D I : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]\n    (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I] [Nontrivial D],\n    IsClosed {u : lp (fun _ : ℕ => D) ∞ | IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun n => u n)}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.familyCauchyAlgebra",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.familyCauchyAlgebra : StarSubalgebra ℂ (lp (fun _ : ℕ => D) ∞) where\n  carrier := {u | IsFamilyCauchy τ (fun n => u n)}\n  zero_mem' := CauchyConstruction.const τ 0\n  one_mem' := CauchyConstruction.const τ 1\n  add_mem' hu hv := CauchyConstruction.add τ hu hv\n  mul_mem' {u v} hu hv := CauchyConstruction.mul τ hu hv\n    (lp.norm_apply_le_norm ENNReal.top_ne_zero u)\n    (lp.norm_apply_le_norm ENNReal.top_ne_zero v)\n  algebraMap_mem' z := CauchyConstruction.const τ (algebraMap ℂ D z)\n  star_mem' hu := CauchyConstruction.star τ hu"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "CurrentMain.familyCauchy_complete",
      "statement_lean": "instance CurrentMain.familyCauchy_complete : CompleteSpace (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) :=\n  (CauchyConstruction.closed τ).completeSpace_coe"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "CurrentMain.familyCauchy_cstar",
      "statement_lean": "instance CurrentMain.familyCauchy_cstar : CStarAlgebra (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) where"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.familyCauchyNullIdeal",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.familyCauchyNullIdeal : ClosedStarIdeal (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) where\n  ideal := TwoSidedIdeal.comap (familyCauchyAlgebra τ).val.toRingHom\n    (familyNullIdeal τ atTop).ideal\n  star_mem := by\n    intro u hu\n    apply (familyNullIdeal τ atTop).star_mem hu\n  isClosed := by\n    exact (familyNullIdeal τ atTop).isClosed.preimage\n      (continuous_subtype_val : Continuous (fun u : familyCauchyAlgebra τ => u.1))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "abbrev",
      "name": "CurrentMain.UniformTracialCompletion",
      "statement_lean": "abbrev CurrentMain.UniformTracialCompletion := (familyCauchyNullIdeal τ).Quotient"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.quotientOrder",
      "statement_lean": "instance Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.quotientOrder (J : ClosedStarIdeal D) : PartialOrder J.Quotient :=\n  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder _"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.quotientStarOrdered",
      "statement_lean": "instance Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.quotientStarOrdered (J : ClosedStarIdeal D) : StarOrderedRing J.Quotient :=\n  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing _"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.descendedValue",
      "statement_lean": "def Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.descendedValue : J.Quotient → ℂ := Quotient.lift τ.functional (by\n  intro u v huv\n  change J.ideal.ringCon u v at huv\n  rw [TwoSidedIdeal.rel_iff] at huv\n  have h := hτ (u-v) huv\n  rw [map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h\n  exact h)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.descendedLinear",
      "statement_lean": "def Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.descendedLinear : J.Quotient →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ where\n  toFun := J.descendedValue τ hτ\n  map_add' x y := Quotient.inductionOn₂' x y (fun u v => τ.functional.map_add u v)\n  map_smul' c x := Quotient.inductionOn' x (fun u => τ.functional.map_smul c u)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.descendedTrace",
      "statement_lean": "def Pureness.ClosedStarIdeal.descendedTrace : TracialState J.Quotient where\n  functional := { J.descendedLinear τ hτ with\n    monotone' := by\n      intro x y hxy\n      apply sub_nonneg.mp\n      change 0 ≤ J.descendedLinear τ hτ y - J.descendedLinear τ hτ x\n      rw [← map_sub]\n      obtain ⟨z,hz⟩ := CStarAlgebra.nonneg_iff_eq_star_mul_self.mp (sub_nonneg.mpr hxy)\n      rw [hz]\n      induction z using Quotient.inductionOn' with\n      | h u => exact τ.functional.map_nonneg (star_mul_self_nonneg u) }\n  normalized := τ.normalized\n  tracial x y := Quotient.inductionOn₂' x y (fun u v => τ.tracial u v)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "CurrentMain.boundedFamilyProductOrder",
      "statement_lean": "instance CurrentMain.boundedFamilyProductOrder : PartialOrder (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) :=\n  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder _"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "CurrentMain.boundedFamilyProductStarOrdered",
      "statement_lean": "instance CurrentMain.boundedFamilyProductStarOrdered : StarOrderedRing (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) :=\n  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing _"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.ultraTraceValue",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.ultraTraceValue (u : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) : ℂ :=\n  limUnder (U : Filter X) (fun n => (θ n).functional (u n))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "class",
      "name": "CurrentMain.LimitConstruction",
      "statement_lean": "class CurrentMain.LimitConstruction : Prop where\n  tendsto : ∀ {D X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D]\n    (θ : X → Pureness.TracialState D) (U : Ultrafilter X) (u : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞),\n    Tendsto (fun n => (θ n).functional (u n)) (U : Filter X) (𝓝 (ultraTraceValue θ U u))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.ultraTraceLinear",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.ultraTraceLinear : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞ →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ where\n  toFun := ultraTraceValue θ U\n  map_add' u v := by\n    apply tendsto_nhds_unique (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U (u+v))\n    simpa only [lp.coeFn_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add] using\n      (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U u).add (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U v)\n  map_smul' z u := by\n    apply tendsto_nhds_unique (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U (z • u))\n    simpa only [lp.coeFn_smul, Pi.smul_apply, map_smul, RingHom.id_apply, smul_eq_mul] using\n      (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U u).const_mul z"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.boundedFamilyEval",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.boundedFamilyEval (n : X) : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞ →⋆ₐ[ℂ] D where\n  toFun u := u n\n  map_zero' := rfl\n  map_one' := rfl\n  map_add' _ _ := rfl\n  map_mul' _ _ := rfl\n  commutes' _ := rfl\n  map_star' _ := rfl"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.ultraTrace",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.ultraTrace : TracialState (lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) where\n  functional := { ultraTraceLinear θ U with\n    monotone' := by\n      intro u v huv\n      exact le_of_tendsto_of_tendsto (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U u)\n        (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U v) (Eventually.of_forall (fun n =>\n          (θ n).functional.monotone (map_rel (boundedFamilyEval n) huv))) }\n  normalized := by\n    apply tendsto_nhds_unique (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U 1)\n    have he (n : X) : (θ n).functional ((1 : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞) n) = 1 :=\n      (θ n).normalized\n    simp only [he]\n    exact tendsto_const_nhds\n  tracial u v := by\n    change ultraTraceValue θ U (u*v) = ultraTraceValue θ U (v*u)\n    apply tendsto_nhds_unique (LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U (u*v))\n    have he (n : X) : (θ n).functional ((u*v) n) = (θ n).functional ((v*u) n) :=\n      (θ n).tracial (u n) (v n)\n    simpa only [he] using LimitConstruction.tendsto θ U (v*u)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "abbrev",
      "name": "CurrentMain.FamilyUltrapower",
      "statement_lean": "abbrev CurrentMain.FamilyUltrapower := (familyNullIdeal τ (U : Filter X)).Quotient"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "class",
      "name": "CurrentMain.TraceConstruction",
      "statement_lean": "class CurrentMain.TraceConstruction : Prop where\n  null : ∀ {D I X : Type} [CStarAlgebra D] [PartialOrder D] [StarOrderedRing D] [Nontrivial D] (τ : I → Pureness.TracialState D) [Nonempty I]\n    (U : Ultrafilter X) (s : X → I) (u : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞),\n    IsFamilyNull τ (U : Filter X) (fun n => u n) →\n      (ultraTrace (fun n => τ (s n)) U).functional u = 0"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.familyLimitTrace",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.familyLimitTrace (s : X → I) : TracialState (FamilyUltrapower τ U) :=\n  (familyNullIdeal τ (U : Filter X)).descendedTrace\n    (ultraTrace (fun n => τ (s n)) U) (fun u hu => by\n      apply TraceConstruction.null τ U s u\n      have he : u ∈ familyNullTwoSided τ (U : Filter X) ↔\n          IsFamilyNull τ (U : Filter X) (fun n => u n) := by\n        unfold familyNullTwoSided\n        exact TwoSidedIdeal.mem_mk' _ _ _ _ _ _ u\n      exact he.mp hu)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.pullbackTrace",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.pullbackTrace (τ : TracialState E) (ι : D →⋆ₐ[ℂ] E) : TracialState D where\n  functional := { τ.functional.toLinearMap.comp ι.toLinearMap with\n    monotone' := fun _ _ h => τ.functional.monotone (map_rel ι h) }\n  normalized := by change τ.functional (ι 1) = 1; rw [map_one, τ.normalized]\n  tracial x y := by\n    change τ.functional (ι (x*y)) = τ.functional (ι (y*x))\n    rw [map_mul ι x y, map_mul ι y x]\n    exact τ.tracial _ _"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "CurrentMain.familyCauchyOrder",
      "statement_lean": "instance CurrentMain.familyCauchyOrder : PartialOrder (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) :=\n  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrder _"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "CurrentMain.familyCauchyStarOrdered",
      "statement_lean": "instance CurrentMain.familyCauchyStarOrdered : StarOrderedRing (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) :=\n  CStarAlgebra.spectralOrderedRing _"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.cauchyTrace",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.cauchyTrace (i : I) : TracialState (familyCauchyAlgebra τ) :=\n  pullbackTrace (ultraTrace (fun _ : ℕ => τ i) (Ultrafilter.of atTop))\n    (familyCauchyAlgebra τ).subtype"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.completionTrace",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.completionTrace (i : I) : TracialState (UniformTracialCompletion τ) :=\n  (familyCauchyNullIdeal τ).descendedTrace (cauchyTrace τ i) (fun u hu => by\n    apply TraceConstruction.null τ (Ultrafilter.of atTop) (fun _ => i) u.1\n    have he : u.1 ∈ familyNullTwoSided τ atTop ↔\n        IsFamilyNull τ atTop (fun n => u.1 n) := by\n      unfold familyNullTwoSided\n      exact TwoSidedIdeal.mem_mk' _ _ _ _ _ _ u.1\n    have hn : IsFamilyNull τ atTop (fun n => u.1 n) := he.mp hu\n    exact hn.mono_left (Ultrafilter.of_le atTop))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CurrentMain.boundedFamilyConstant",
      "statement_lean": "def CurrentMain.boundedFamilyConstant {X : Type} (a : D) : lp (fun _ : X => D) ∞ :=\n  ⟨fun _ => a, memℓp_infty_iff.mpr ⟨‖a‖,by rintro _ ⟨n,rfl⟩; exact le_rfl⟩⟩"
    },
```

<a id="pm-s7"></a>
## 7. Complete mathematical paper


For a simple, separable, unital, infinite-dimensional, nuclear, stably finite <i>C</i><sup>∗</sup>-algebra with traces, real rank zero of the uniform tracial ultrapower of its uniform tracial completion implies uniform property Γ. This answers Problem XXI of Schafhauser, Tikuisis and White affirmatively. Independently, strict comparison implies Jiang–Su absorption for simple, separable, unital, infinite-dimensional nuclear algebras, resolving the unital Toms–Winter conjecture. Under comparison tested on traces of finite target rank, we also obtain uniform property Γ and absorption for simple, separable, nuclear, stably projectionless algebras whose densely finite traces are all bounded and have a nonempty compact normalized base.
